The ninth term, the one-loop correction shown in frame (12) of Figure
B.2
, gives

\displaystyle-iev^{2}p_{\mu}\langle\rho(p)hA_{v}(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-iev^{2}\Bigg[-\frac{ie\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}

(B.58)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}\Big(ie\left(-\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}(-\xi)+p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m_{h}^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left.\left(\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}+p^{2}\right){}^{2}+4m^{2}p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)-m^{2}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\left.m_{h}^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)-p^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)+m^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)\right)\Big)\Bigg]\mathcal{L}_{\mu v}.

The tenth term, the one-loop correction shown in frame (14) of Figure
B.2
, gives

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}vp_{\mu}p_{\nu}\langle h\rho(p)\rho(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}v\Bigg[p^{2}\Big(-\frac{e^{2}vm_{h}^{2}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m_{h}^{2}\right)}{m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}-\frac{(d-1)e^{2}v\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}

(B.59)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{3e^{2}v\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{2m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}-\frac{e^{2}\xi v\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}-\frac{e^{2}v\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}+\frac{em\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}\Big)\Bigg]\mathcal{L}_{\mu v}.

The eleventh term, the one-loop correction shown in frame (15) of Figure
B.2
, gives

\displaystyle ivp_{\nu}\langle\partial^{x}{}_{\mu}h\rho(p)\rho(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle iv\Big[\frac{ie^{2}m_{h}^{2}v\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m^{4}\xi+2m^{2}p^{2}}-\frac{ie^{2}m_{h}^{2}v\left(\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}\xi-p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m_{h}^{2}\right)+\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)\right)}{2m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}\Big]\mathcal{L}_{\mu v}.

(B.60)

The twelfth term, the one-loop correction shown in frame (16) of Figure
B.2
, gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}p_{\mu}p_{\nu}\langle hh(p)\rho\rho(-p)\rangle=p^{2}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m_{h}^{2}\right)\mathcal{L}_{\mu v}.

(B.61)

The thirteenth term, the one-loop correction shown in frame (17) of Figure
B.2
, gives

\displaystyle-\langle\partial^{x}{}_{\mu}h\rho(p)h\partial^{y}{}_{\nu}\rho(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big[\frac{\left(\frac{\left(-m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right){}^{2}}{p^{2}}+4m_{h}^{2}\right)\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)}{4(d-1)}+\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\left(-m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}\xi-p^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}}

(B.62)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)\left(-m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}}\Big]\mathcal{P}_{\mu v}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\Big[-\frac{\left(m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}(-\xi)-p^{2}\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}(-\xi)+p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m_{h}^{2}\right)}{4p^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}(-\xi)-p^{2}\right)}{4p^{2}}-\frac{\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}(-\xi)+p^{2}\right)}{4p^{2}}\Big]\mathcal{L}_{\mu v}.

The fourteenth term, the one-loop correction shown in frame (18) of Figure
B.2
, gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}v^{2}p_{\mu}p_{\nu}\langle\rho(p)\rho(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[p^{2}\big(\frac{e^{4}v^{2}m_{h}^{4}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m_{h}^{2}\right)}{m^{4}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{e^{2}\left(\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}+p^{2}\right){}^{2}+4m^{2}p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)}{m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

(B.63)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right){}^{2}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)}{m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{(d-1)e^{4}v^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{(d-1)e^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{e^{4}v^{2}m_{h}^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m^{4}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{3e^{4}v^{2}m_{h}^{2}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{2m^{4}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{e^{4}\xi v^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{e^{3}\xi v\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{m\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{e^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}{m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{e^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}+p^{2}\right)}{m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{3e^{2}m_{h}^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{e^{2}m_{h}^{2}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{2m^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2}\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\big)\Big]\mathcal{L}_{\mu v}.

Appendix C
Technical details to the
SU(2)
Higgs model

C.1
Propagators and vertices of the
SU(2)
Higgs model in the
R_{\xi}
gauge

The tree level elementary propagators of the fields are easily computed, being given by

\displaystyle\langle A^{a}_{\mu}(p)A^{b}_{v}(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{ab}}{p^{2}+m^{2}}\mathcal{P}_{\mu v}(p)+\delta^{ab}\frac{\xi}{p^{2}+\xi m^{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\mu v}(p),

\displaystyle\langle\rho^{a}(p)\rho^{b}(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{ab}}{p^{2}+\xi m^{2}},

\displaystyle\langle h(p)h(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p^{2}+m_{h}^{2}},

\displaystyle\langle A^{a}_{\mu}(p)b^{b}(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{ab}\frac{p_{\mu}}{p^{2}+\xi m^{2}},

\displaystyle\langle b^{a}(p)\rho^{b}(-k)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{ab}\frac{im}{p^{2}+\xi m^{2}}

(C.1)

and

\displaystyle\langle\bar{c}^{a}(p)c^{b}(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{ab}}{p^{2}+\xi m^{2}}

(C.2)

for the ghost propagator.
For all vertices, adopting the convention that the momentum is flowing towards the vertex, we get

•

The
AAh
-vertex:

\Gamma_{A_{\mu}^{a}A_{\nu}^{b}h}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3})=-\frac{g^{2}v}{2}\delta_{\mu\nu}\delta^{ab}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})
.

•

The
\rho\rho A
-vertex:
\Gamma_{\rho^{a}\rho^{b}A_{\mu}^{c}}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3})=\frac{g}{2}i\epsilon^{abc}(p_{\mu,1}-p_{\mu,2})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})
.

•

The
A\rho h
-vertex:
\Gamma_{A_{\mu}^{a}\rho^{b}h}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3})=i\frac{g}{2}\delta^{ab}(p_{\mu,3}-p_{\mu,2})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})
.

•

The
hhh
vertex:
\Gamma_{hhh}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3})=-3\lambda v\,\,\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})
.

•

The
h\rho\rho
vertex:
\Gamma_{h\rho^{a}\rho^{b}}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3})=-\lambda v\delta^{ab}\,\,\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})
.

•

The
AAA
-vertex:
\Gamma_{A_{\mu}^{a}A_{v}^{b}A_{\sigma}^{c}}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3})=-igf^{abc}\left[(p_{1}-p_{3})_{\nu}\delta_{\sigma\mu}+(p_{3}-p_{2})_{\mu}\delta_{v\sigma}+(p_{2}-p_{1})_{\sigma}\delta_{v\mu}\right]\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})
.

•

The
\overline{c}Ac
-vertex:
\Gamma_{\overline{c}^{a}A_{\mu}^{b}c^{c}}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3})=igf^{abc}p_{1,\mu}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3})
.

•

The
AAAA
-vertex:
\Gamma_{A_{\mu}^{a}A_{\nu}^{b}A_{\rho}^{c}A_{\sigma}^{d}}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3},-p_{4})=g^{2}[f^{eab}f^{ecd}(\delta_{\mu\sigma}\delta_{\nu\rho}-\delta_{\mu\rho}\delta_{\nu\sigma})+f^{eac}f^{ebd}(\delta_{\mu\sigma}\delta_{\nu\rho}-\delta_{\mu\nu}\delta_{\rho\sigma})+f^{ead}f^{ebc}(\delta_{\mu\rho}\delta_{\nu\sigma}-\delta_{\mu\nu}\delta_{\rho\sigma})]\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}+p_{4})
.

•

The
AAhh
-vertex:
\Gamma_{A_{\mu}^{a}A_{v}^{b}hh}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3},-p_{4})=-\frac{1}{2}g^{2}\delta^{ab}\delta_{\mu v}
.

•

The
AA\rho\rho
-vertex:
\Gamma_{A_{\mu}^{a}A_{v}^{b}\rho^{c}\rho^{d}}(-p_{1},-p_{2},-p_{3},-p_{4})=-\frac{1}{2}g^{2}\delta_{\mu v}\delta^{ab}\delta^{cd}
.

C.2
Elementary propagators of the
SU(2)
Higgs model in the
R_{\xi}
gauge

Here we will calculate the one-loop corrections to the Higgs and gauge field propagator. This requires the calculation
1
1

1

We have used from (
??
) the technique of modifying integrals into “master integrals” without numerators.
of the Feynman diagrams as shown in Figures
C.1
and
C.2
. We will use the following definitions:

\displaystyle\eta(m_{1},m_{2})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{d/2}}\Gamma(2-\frac{d}{2})\int_{0}^{1}dx\left(p^{2}x(1-x)+xm_{1}+(1-x)m_{2}\right)^{d/2-2},

\displaystyle\chi(m_{1})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{d/2}}\Gamma(1-\frac{d}{2})m_{1}^{d/2-1}.

(C.3)

Notice that the last four diagrams for both particles are zero for
\langle h\rangle=0
. In fact, including these diagrams has the same effect as making a shift in the minimizing value of the scalar field
\Phi
to demand
\langle h\rangle=0
, see the Appendix of (
??
) for the technical details. In the context of the FMS operators, we found it more convenient to expand around the (classical)
v
that is gauge invariant, and thus to include the tadpoles. Expanding the FMS operator around the quantum corrected vev would lead to cancellations in that quantum vev coming from the propagator loop corrections to render it gauge invariant again, indeed the minimum of the quantum corrected effective Higgs potential is not gauge invariant itself.

C.2.1
Higgs propagator

Figure C.1:
One-loop contributions to the propagator
\langle h(p)h(-p)\rangle
. Curly lines represent the gauge field, dashed lines the Higgs field, solid lines the Goldstone boson and double lines the ghost field.

The first diagrams contributing to the Higgs self-energy are of the snail type, renormalizing the masses of the internal fields.
The Higgs boson snail (first diagram in the first line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,1}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3\lambda\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{2\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}},

(C.4)

the Goldstone boson snail (second diagram in the first line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,2}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3\lambda\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}},

(C.5)

and the gauge field snail (third diagram in the first line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,3}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3(d-1)g^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{4\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{3g^{2}\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{4\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

(C.6)

Next, we meet a couple of sunset diagrams. The Higgs boson sunset (fourth diagram in the first line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,4}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{9\lambda^{2}v^{2}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)}{2\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\Big\},

(C.7)

the gauge field sunset (first diagram in the second line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,5}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3g^{2}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)\left(4(D-1)m^{4}+4m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{8m^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{3g^{2}\left(2m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)}{8m^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

(C.8)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{3g^{2}\left(m^{4}(\xi-1)^{2}+2m^{2}\xi p^{2}+2m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)}{4m^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{3g^{2}(\xi-1)\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{4\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{3g^{2}(\xi-1)\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{4\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\Big\},

the ghost sunset (second diagram in the second line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,6}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3g^{2}m^{2}\xi^{2}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)}{4\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\Big\},

(C.9)

the Goldstone boson sunset (third diagram in the second line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,7}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3\lambda^{2}v^{2}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)}{2\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\Big\},

(C.10)

and a mixed Goldstone-gauge sunset (fourth diagram in the second line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,8}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3g^{2}\left(\left(m^{2}(\xi-1)+p^{2}\right)^{2}+4m^{2}p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)}{4m^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{3g^{2}\left(m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)}{4m^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

(C.11)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{3g^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\left(m^{2}(2\xi-1)+p^{2}\right)}{4m^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{3g^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}(\xi-1)+p^{2}\right)}{4m^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\Big\}.

Finally, we have the tadpole diagrams. The Higgs balloon (first diagram on the third line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,9}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9\lambda^{2}v^{2}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}},

(C.12)

the gauge balloon (second diagram on the third line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,10}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9g\lambda mv\left((d-1)\chi\left(m^{2}\right)+\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}},

(C.13)

the Goldstone boson balloon (third diagram on the third line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,11}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9\lambda^{2}v^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}},

(C.14)

the ghost balloon (fourth diagram on the third line of Figure
C.1
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{hh,12}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{9g\lambda m\xi v\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}}.

(C.15)

Putting together eqs. (
C.4
) to (
C.15
) we find the Higgs propagator up to first order in
\hbar

\displaystyle\braket{h(x)\,h(y)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p^{2}+m_{h}^{2}}+g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3\left(4(d-1)m^{4}+4m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{8m^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)

(C.16)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{9m_{h}^{4}}{8m^{2}}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)+\frac{3\left(m_{h}^{4}-p^{4}\right)}{8m^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)+\frac{\left(6(d-1)m^{2}-3p^{2}\right)}{4m^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{3\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)}{4m^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)+\frac{3m_{h}^{2}}{4m^{2}}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)\Big\}\frac{1}{(p^{2}+m_{h}^{2})^{2}}.

The resummed one-loop Higgs propagator can be now approximated by

\displaystyle G^{-1}_{hh}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p^{2}+m_{h}^{2}-g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3\left(4(d-1)m^{4}+4m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{8m^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)

(C.17)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{9m_{h}^{4}}{8m^{2}}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)+\frac{3\left(m_{h}^{4}-p^{4}\right)}{8m^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)+\frac{\left(6(d-1)m^{2}-3p^{2}\right)}{4m^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{3\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)}{4m^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)+\frac{3m_{h}^{2}}{4m^{2}}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)\Big\}.

For
d=4
, the above expression, eq. (
C.17
), is divergent. Employing the procedure of dimensional regularization,
i.e.
setting
d=4-\epsilon
, the divergent part for
G_{hh}(p^{2})
is given by:

\displaystyle G_{hh,\mathrm{d}iv}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{g^{2}\left(\frac{3m_{h}^{4}}{m^{2}}-3\xi m_{h}^{2}-3\xi p^{2}+9p^{2}\right)}{32\pi^{2}\epsilon},

(C.18)

which, following the
\overline{MS}bar
-scheme, are re-absorbed by the introduction of suitable local counterterms. We remain thus with the finite part of the Higgs propagator, given in eq. (
5.21
).

C.2.2
Gauge field propagator

Figure C.2:
Contributions to the one-loop gauge field self-energy.

The first diagram contributing to transverse part of the gauge field self-energy is the gauge field snail (first diagram in the first line of Figure
C.2
) and gives a contribution:

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},1}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2g^{2}\left(p^{2}-d\left(d^{2}-3d+3\right)p^{2}\right)\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{(d-1)dp^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{2g^{2}\xi\left((d-2)dp^{2}+p^{2}\right)\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{(d-1)dp^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

(C.19)

The second diagram is the Goldstone boson snail (second diagram in the first line of Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},2}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3g^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{4\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

(C.20)

The third diagram is the Higgs boson snail (third diagram in the first line of Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},3}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{g^{2}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{4\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

(C.21)

The fourth diagram is the gauge field sunrise (first diagram in the second line of Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},4}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)\left(2m^{2}p^{2}(-2d+\xi+3)+m^{4}(\xi-1)^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{2(d-1)m^{4}p^{2}}

(C.22)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\left(4m^{2}+p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)\left(4(d-1)m^{4}+4(3-2d)m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{4(d-1)m^{4}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\left(4m^{2}\xi p^{4}+p^{6}\right)\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)}{4(d-1)m^{4}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\left(4d^{2}\left(m^{2}(\xi+1)p^{2}+p^{4}\right)+d\left(m^{4}(\xi-1)-m^{2}(6\xi+7)p^{2}+(\xi-7)p^{4}\right)+4m^{2}\xi p^{2}\right)}{2(d-1)dm^{2}p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\right)\left(4d^{2}p^{4}+d\left(m^{4}(\xi-1)+m^{2}(2\xi-5)p^{2}+(\xi-7)p^{4}\right)+4m^{2}p^{2}\right)}{2(d-1)dm^{2}p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\Big\}.

The fifth diagram is the ghost sunrise (second diagram in the second line of Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},5}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)\left(\frac{2m^{2}\xi}{(d-1)\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{p^{2}}{2(d-1)\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\right)

(C.23)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{(d-1)\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

The sixth diagram is the Goldstone sunrise (first diagram in the third line of Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},6}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)\left(-\frac{m^{2}\xi}{(d-1)\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{p^{2}}{4(d-1)\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}\right)

(C.24)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2(d-1)\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

The seventh diagram is the mixed Goldstone-Higgs sunrise (second diagram in the third line of Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},7}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx-\frac{\left(\left(-m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}+4m_{h}^{2}p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)\left(-m_{h}^{2}+m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

(C.25)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

The eighth diagram is the mixed Goldstone-gauge field sunrise (third diagram in the third line of Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},8}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\frac{\left(\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)^{2}+4m^{2}\xi p^{2}\right)\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)}{4(D-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

(C.26)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\eta\left(m^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)\left(\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}-4(D-2)m^{2}p^{2}\right)}{4(D-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}-\frac{m^{2}(\xi-1)\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{4(D-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}{4(D-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\right)\left(m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}+p^{2}\right)}{4(D-1)p^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

Finally, we have four tadpole (balloon) diagrams. The Higgs boson balloon (first diagram of the last line in Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},5}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3gm\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{2v\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}},

(C.27)

the Goldstone boson balloon (second diagram of the last line in Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},6}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3g\lambda mv\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}},

(C.28)

The gauge field balloon (third diagram of the last line in Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Pi_{AA^{T},7}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3(D-1)g^{2}m^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}+\frac{3g^{2}m^{2}\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}

(C.29)

and finally, the ghost balloon (fourth diagram of the last line in Figure
C.2
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{AA^{T},8}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3g^{2}m^{2}\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{2m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}}.

(C.30)

Combining all these contributions (
C.19
)-(
C.30
), we find the total one-loop correction to the gauge field self-energy

\displaystyle\braket{A^{a}_{\mu}(p)A^{b}_{\nu}(p)}^{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{ab}}{p^{2}+m^{2}}+\delta^{ab}g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{-\frac{\left(2(3-2d)m^{2}p^{2}+m_{h}^{4}-2m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}-p^{2}\right)+m^{4}+p^{4}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)

(C.31)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}\left(2m^{2}p^{2}(-2d+\xi+3)+m^{4}(\xi-1)^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{2(d-1)m^{4}p^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m^{4}-p^{4}\right)\left(4m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)m^{4}}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)-\frac{\left(4m^{2}+p^{2}\right)\left(4(d-1)m^{4}+4(3-2d)m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{4(d-1)m^{4}}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m_{h}^{2}\left(-m^{2}p^{2}\left(8d^{2}-24d+4\xi+13\right)-2p^{4}(4d+\xi-7)+m^{4}(1-2\xi)\right)+6(d-1)^{2}m^{4}p^{2}+m_{h}^{4}m^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)m_{h}^{2}m^{2}p^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\left((d-2)p^{2}+m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)+\frac{\left(m^{2}p^{2}(5d+4\xi-13)+2p^{4}(4d+\xi-7)+2m^{4}(\xi-1)\right)}{4(d-1)m^{2}p^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)+\frac{3}{4}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\Big\}\frac{1}{(p^{2}+m^{2})^{2}}

and the resummed propagator for the transverse gauge field can be approximated, at one-loop order, by

\displaystyle G^{-1}_{AA^{T}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{ab}\Bigg(p^{2}+m^{2}-g^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{-\frac{\left(2(3-2d)m^{2}p^{2}+m_{h}^{4}-2m_{h}^{2}\left(m^{2}-p^{2}\right)+m^{4}+p^{4}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)

(C.32)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m^{2}+p^{2}\right)^{2}\left(2m^{2}p^{2}(-2d+\xi+3)+m^{4}(\xi-1)^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{2(d-1)m^{4}p^{2}}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\xi\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m^{4}-p^{4}\right)\left(4m^{2}\xi+p^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)m^{4}}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)-\frac{\left(4m^{2}+p^{2}\right)\left(4(d-1)m^{4}+4(3-2d)m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{4(d-1)m^{4}}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m_{h}^{2}\left(-m^{2}p^{2}\left(8d^{2}-24d+4\xi+13\right)-2p^{4}(4d+\xi-7)+m^{4}(1-2\xi)\right)+6(d-1)^{2}m^{4}p^{2}+m_{h}^{4}m^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)m_{h}^{2}m^{2}p^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\left((d-2)p^{2}+m_{h}^{2}-m^{2}\right)}{4(d-1)p^{2}}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)+\frac{\left(m^{2}p^{2}(5d+4\xi-13)+2p^{4}(4d+\xi-7)+2m^{4}(\xi-1)\right)}{4(d-1)m^{2}p^{2}}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)+\frac{3}{4}\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)\Big\}\Bigg).

For
d=4-\epsilon
, following the procedure of dimensional regularization, we find that the divergent part for
G_{AA^{T}}(p^{2})
is given by:

\displaystyle G_{AA^{T},\mathrm{d}iv}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{g^{2}}{\pi^{2}\epsilon}\left(-\frac{9m^{4}}{16m_{h}^{2}}-\frac{3m_{h}^{2}}{32}-\frac{m^{2}\xi}{8}-\frac{3m^{2}}{32}-\frac{\xi p^{2}}{8}+\frac{25p^{2}}{48}\right),

(C.33)

and these terms can be, following the
\overline{MS}bar
-scheme, absorbed by means of appropriate counterterms. We remain with the finite part of the propagator, given in eq. (
5.31
).

C.3
Contributions to
\langle O(p)O(-p)\rangle

The diagrams which contribute to the correlation function
\langle O(p)O(-p)\rangle
are depicted in Figure(
C.3
)

Figure C.3:
One-loop contributions to the correlation function
\langle OO\rangle
. Wavy lines represent the gauge field, dashed lines the Higgs field, solid lines the Goldstone boson and double lines the ghost field. The
\bullet
indicates the insertion of a composite operator.

.

The first term is
v^{2}
times the one-loop correction to the Higgs propagator, given in eq. (
C.17
). The second term is

\displaystyle v\langle h(p)(\rho^{a}\rho^{a})(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-3\frac{m_{h}^{2}\eta\left(m^{2}\xi,m^{2}\xi\right)}{m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}}.

(C.34)

The third term is

\displaystyle v\langle h(p)h^{2}(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3m_{h}^{2}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)}{m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}}-\frac{3\chi\left(m_{h}^{2}\right)}{m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}}-\frac{\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}}-\frac{2(D-1)m^{2}\chi\left(m^{2}\right)}{m_{h}^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)}-\frac{2m^{2}\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{m_{h}^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)}

(C.35)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{2m^{2}\xi\chi\left(m^{2}\xi\right)}{m_{h}^{2}\left(m_{h}^{2}+p^{2}\right)}.

The fourth term is

\displaystyle\langle m_{h}^{2}(p)m_{h}^{2}(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right).

(C.36)

The fifth term is

\displaystyle\langle(\rho^{a}\rho^{a})(p)(\rho^{b}\rho^{b})(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\eta\left(\xi m^{2},\xi m^{2}\right)

(C.37)

and together these terms give the correlation function of the scalar composite operator
O
up to first order in
\hbar

\displaystyle\langle O(p)O(-p)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{v^{2}}{p^{2}+m_{h}^{2}}+\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3}{2}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)(4(d-1)m^{4}+4m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4})+\frac{1}{2}(p^{2}-2m_{h}^{2})^{2}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)

(C.38)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{3p^{2}\chi(m^{2})(2(d-1)m^{2}+m_{h}^{2})}{m_{h}^{2}}-3p^{2}\chi(m_{h}^{2})\Big\}\frac{1}{(p^{2}+m_{h}^{2})^{2}}.

Thus, for the one-loop resummed correlation function, we get

\displaystyle G^{-1}_{OO}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{p^{2}+m_{h}^{2}}{v^{2}}-\frac{1}{v^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}dx\Big\{\frac{3}{2}\eta\left(m^{2},m^{2}\right)(4(d-1)m^{4}+4m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4})+\frac{1}{2}(p^{2}-2m_{h}^{2})^{2}\eta\left(m_{h}^{2},m_{h}^{2}\right)

(C.39)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{3p^{2}\chi(m^{2})(2(d-1)m^{2}+m_{h}^{2})}{m_{h}^{2}}-3p^{2}\chi(m_{h}^{2})\Big\}\frac{1}{(p^{2}+m_{h}^{2})^{2}}.

Following the procedure of dimensional regularization for
d=4-\epsilon
, we find that the divergent part of the correlator is given by

\displaystyle G_{OO,\mathrm{d}iv}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4v^{4}\pi^{2}\epsilon}\Big(\frac{9g^{4}p^{2}v^{2}}{16\lambda}+\frac{9g^{4}v^{4}}{16}+\frac{9}{8}g^{2}p^{2}v^{2}+p^{4}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda p^{2}v^{2}+\lambda^{2}v^{4}\Big),

(C.40)

which can be accounted for by appropriate counterterm, following the
\overline{MS}bar
-scheme renormalization procedure. We remain with the finite part of the correlator, given in eq. (
5.76
).

C.4
A few comments on the unitary gauge

It is well-known that in the unitary gauge the unphysical fields, like the Goldstone and ghost fields, decouple, a feature which allows for a more direct link with the spectrum of the elementary excitations of the model. However, this gauge is known to be non-renormalizable. In fact, working directly with the elementary tree level propagators taken already in the unitary limit,
i.e.

\xi\rightarrow\infty
, and following the steps of dimensional regularization, we find that the divergent part of the Higgs propagator reads

\displaystyle G^{-1}_{hh,\mathrm{d}iv}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3g^{2}\left(m_{h}^{4}+6m^{2}p^{2}+p^{4}\right)}{64\pi^{2}m^{2}\epsilon}.

(C.41)

In expression (
C.41
) we clearly see the presence of the term
\sim\frac{p^{4}}{\epsilon m^{2}}
, signalling the aforementioned issue of the non-renormalizability. Nevertheless, it is interesting to observe that, if we remove the divergent part (
C.41
) anyway, we obtain the spectral function as shown in Figure
C.4
. This spectral function is almost identical to that obtained for the composite operator
O(x)
, see Figure
5.8
.

Figure C.4:
Spectral function for the propagator
\langle h(p)h(-p)\rangle
in the unitary gauge, with
t
given in unity of
\mu^{2}
, for the Region I (left) and Region II (right), with parameter values given in Table
IV A
.

For the gauge field propagator, proceeding in the same way, we find the divergent part

\displaystyle G^{-1}_{AA,\mathrm{d}iv}(p^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\Big(-\frac{9g^{2}m^{4}}{16\pi^{2}m_{h}^{2}}-\frac{g^{2}p^{6}}{96\pi^{2}m^{4}}+\frac{7g^{2}p^{4}}{48\pi^{2}m^{2}}+\frac{3g^{2}m^{2}}{32\pi^{2}}+\frac{83g^{2}p^{2}}{96\pi^{2}}-\frac{3\lambda m^{2}}{8\pi^{2}}\Big)

(C.42)

which shows again the non-renormalizability of unitary gauge, through the terms
\sim\frac{p^{6}}{\epsilon m^{4}}
and
\sim\frac{p^{4}}{\epsilon m^{2}}
. However, if we remove again those terms anyway, we obtain the spectral function as shown in Figure
C.5
. Nevertheless, as already remarked in the previous sections, this nice behaviour of the spectral densities for the Higgs and gauge field obtained by a direct use of the tree level propagators already taken in the unitary limit,
\xi\rightarrow\infty
, can be, to some extent, justified by the fact that we are working at the one-loop order in perturbation theory. Since overlapping divergences start from one-loop onward, we can easily figure out that the naive use of the elementary tree level propagators taken already in the unitary limit will run into severe non-renormalizibility issues, making the removal of the (overlapping) divergent parts (
C.41
), (
C.42
) quite problematic.

Figure C.5:
Spectral function for the propagator
\langle A^{a}_{\mu}(p)A^{b}_{\nu}(-p)\rangle
in the unitary gauge, with
t
given in unity of
\mu^{2}
, for the Region I (left) and Region II (right), with parameter values given in Table
IV A
.
